-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        for (int j = i + 1; j < width; ++j) a[row * n + offset + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateFinalTile(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining < kBlockSize)
+            updateFinalTile<<<1, dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }
+} // namespace

+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateFinalTile(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining < kBlockSize)
+            updateFinalTile<<<1, dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }
+} // namespace

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;

+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {

+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;

+__global__ void updateFinalTile(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];

+}

+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }

+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining < kBlockSize)
+            updateFinalTile<<<1, dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }

-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// A right-looking blocked Cholesky decomposition.  The matrix stays on the GPU
+// for the complete factorization; only the final lower factor is copied back.
+namespace {
+constexpr int kBlockSize = 32;
+
+bool cudaOk(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return true;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));

-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

+__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int width, int* failed) {
+    // A diagonal block is deliberately processed serially.  It is small, and
+    // this avoids global synchronization inside its dependency chain.
+    if (threadIdx.x != 0 || blockIdx.x != 0) return;
+    for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = offset + static_cast<size_t>(i);
+        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
             double sum = 0.0;
-

+                const double x = a[row * n + offset + p];
+                sum += x * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+            }
+            const size_t index = row * n + offset + j;
             if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];

-                    return false;
+                const double value = a[index] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    *failed = 1;
+                    return;
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                a[index] = sqrt(value);

-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];

+        for (int j = i + 1; j < width; ++j) a[row * n + offset + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateFinalTile(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining < kBlockSize)
+            updateFinalTile<<<1, dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }

+namespace {
+constexpr int kBlockSize = 32;
+
+bool cudaOk(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return true;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));

-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

+__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int width, int* failed) {
+    // A diagonal block is deliberately processed serially.  It is small, and
+    // this avoids global synchronization inside its dependency chain.
+    if (threadIdx.x != 0 || blockIdx.x != 0) return;
+    for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = offset + static_cast<size_t>(i);
+        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
             double sum = 0.0;
-            
+            for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p) {
+                const double x = a[row * n + offset + p];
+                sum += x * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+            }
+            const size_t index = row * n + offset + j;
             if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+                const double value = a[index] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    *failed = 1;
+                    return;
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                a[index] = sqrt(value);
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                a[index] = (a[index] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
             }

-

-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        for (int j = i + 1; j < width; ++j) a[row * n + offset + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateBoundaryTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining % kBlockSize != 0)
+            updateBoundaryTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }
+} // namespace

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// A right-looking blocked Cholesky decomposition.  The matrix stays on the GPU
+// for the complete factorization; only the final lower factor is copied back.
+namespace {
+constexpr int kBlockSize = 32;
+
+bool cudaOk(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return true;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    return false;

-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int width, int* failed) {
+    // A diagonal block is deliberately processed serially.  It is small, and
+    // this avoids global synchronization inside its dependency chain.
+    if (threadIdx.x != 0 || blockIdx.x != 0) return;
+    for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = offset + static_cast<size_t>(i);
+        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
             double sum = 0.0;
-            
+            for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p) {
+                const double x = a[row * n + offset + p];
+                sum += x * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+            }
+            const size_t index = row * n + offset + j;
             if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+                const double value = a[index] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    *failed = 1;
+                    return;
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                a[index] = sqrt(value);
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                a[index] = (a[index] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        for (int j = i + 1; j < width; ++j) a[row * n + offset + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateBoundaryTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining % kBlockSize != 0)
+            updateBoundaryTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }
+} // namespace

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// A right-looking blocked Cholesky decomposition.  The matrix stays on the GPU
+// for the complete factorization; only the final lower factor is copied back.
+namespace {
+constexpr int kBlockSize = 32;
+
+bool cudaOk(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return true;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    return false;

-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int width, int* failed) {
+    // A diagonal block is deliberately processed serially.  It is small, and
+    // this avoids global synchronization inside its dependency chain.
+    if (threadIdx.x != 0 || blockIdx.x != 0) return;
+    for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = offset + static_cast<size_t>(i);
+        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
             double sum = 0.0;
-            
+            for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p) {
+                const double x = a[row * n + offset + p];
+                sum += x * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+            }
+            const size_t index = row * n + offset + j;
             if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+                const double value = a[index] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    *failed = 1;
+                    return;
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                a[index] = sqrt(value);
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                a[index] = (a[index] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        for (int j = i + 1; j < width; ++j) a[row * n + offset + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateBoundaryTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining % kBlockSize != 0)
+            updateBoundaryTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }
+} // namespace

                          specify how differences in submodules are shown
    --ita-invisible-in-index
                          hide 'git add -N' entries from the index
    --ita-visible-in-index
                          treat 'git add -N' entries as real in the index
    -S <string>           look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified string
    -G <regex>            look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified regex
    --pickaxe-all         show all changes in the changeset with -S or -G
    --pickaxe-regex       treat <string> in -S as extended POSIX regular expression
    -O <file>             control the order in which files appear in the output
    --rotate-to <path>    show the change in the specified path first
    --skip-to <path>      skip the output to the specified path
    --find-object <object-id>
                          look for differences that change the number of occurrences of the specified object
    --diff-filter [(A|C|D|M|R|T|U|X|B)...[*]]
                          select files by diff type
    --output <file>       output to a specific file

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+// A right-looking blocked Cholesky decomposition.  The matrix stays on the GPU
+// for the complete factorization; only the final lower factor is copied back.
+namespace {
+constexpr int kBlockSize = 32;
+
+bool cudaOk(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {
+    if (error == cudaSuccess) return true;
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));

-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
+__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int width, int* failed) {
+    // A diagonal block is deliberately processed serially.  It is small, and
+    // this avoids global synchronization inside its dependency chain.
+    if (threadIdx.x != 0 || blockIdx.x != 0) return;
+    for (int i = 0; i < width; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = offset + static_cast<size_t>(i);
+        for (int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
             double sum = 0.0;
-            
+            for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p) {
+                const double x = a[row * n + offset + p];
+                sum += x * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+            }
+            const size_t index = row * n + offset + j;
             if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
+                const double value = a[index] - sum;
+                if (value <= 0.0) {
+                    *failed = 1;
+                    return;
                 }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
+                a[index] = sqrt(value);
             } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
+                a[index] = (a[index] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
             }
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        for (int j = i + 1; j < width; ++j) a[row * n + offset + j] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateBoundaryTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining % kBlockSize != 0)
+            updateBoundaryTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }
+} // namespace

+__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int width, int* failed) {

+
+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateBoundaryTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, a.data(), a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < n; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(kBlockSize), n - offset));
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = n - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        if (remaining % kBlockSize != 0)
+            updateBoundaryTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(kBlockSize, kBlockSize)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        else
+            updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, n, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((n + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, n);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(a.data(), deviceA, a.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
 }
+} // namespace

+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();

+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;

-// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* a, size_t n) {
+    const size_t col = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && col > row) a[row * n + col] = 0.0;
+}
+
+__global__ void updateBoundaryTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t row = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    const size_t col = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n || col >= n || row < col) return;
+    double sum = 0.0;
+    for (int p = 0; p < diagonalWidth; ++p)
+        sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[col * n + offset + p];
+    a[row * n + col] -= sum;
+}
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, float& elapsedMs) {
+    if (n == 0) { elapsedMs = 0.0f; return true; }
+    // Pad to complete tiles with an identity block.  This keeps every GPU
+    // update regular while leaving the requested leading principal factor
+    // mathematically unchanged.
+    const size_t workN = ((n + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize) * kBlockSize;
+    std::vector<double> padded(workN * workN, 0.0);
+    for (size_t row = 0; row < n; ++row)
+        std::copy_n(a.data() + row * n, n, padded.data() + row * workN);
+    for (size_t row = n; row < workN; ++row) padded[row * workN + row] = 1.0;
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    int* deviceFailed = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t start = nullptr, stop = nullptr;
+    bool success = false;
+    int failed = 0;
+
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceA, padded.size() * sizeof(double)), "matrix allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMalloc(&deviceFailed, sizeof(int)), "status allocation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(deviceA, padded.data(), padded.size() * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "matrix upload") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaMemset(deviceFailed, 0, sizeof(int)), "status initialization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&start), "start event creation") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventCreate(&stop), "stop event creation")) goto cleanup;
+
+    cudaEventRecord(start);
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < workN; offset += kBlockSize) {
+        const int width = kBlockSize;
+        factorDiagonalBlock<<<1, 1>>>(deviceA, workN, offset, width, deviceFailed);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "diagonal block factorization")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const size_t remaining = workN - offset - width;
+        if (remaining == 0) continue;
+        solvePanel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + 255) / 256), 256>>>(deviceA, workN, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, workN, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((workN + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((workN + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, workN);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy2D(a.data(), n * sizeof(double), deviceA, workN * sizeof(double),
+                             n * sizeof(double), n, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
+}
+} // namespace

+    // this avoids global synchronization inside its dependency chain.

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)

+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];

+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;

+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "panel solve")) goto cleanup;
+
+        const unsigned tiles = static_cast<unsigned>((remaining + kBlockSize - 1) / kBlockSize);
+        updateTrailing<<<dim3(tiles, tiles), dim3(16, 16)>>>(deviceA, workN, offset, width);
+        if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "trailing update")) goto cleanup;
+    }
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<dim3(static_cast<unsigned>((workN + 31) / 32), static_cast<unsigned>((workN + 31) / 32)), dim3(32, 32)>>>(deviceA, workN);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaGetLastError(), "upper triangle cleanup")) goto cleanup;
+    cudaEventRecord(stop);
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaEventSynchronize(stop), "factorization synchronization") ||
+        !cudaOk(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMs, start, stop), "elapsed-time measurement")) goto cleanup;
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy(&failed, deviceFailed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "status download")) goto cleanup;
+    if (failed) {
+        std::printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite\n");
+        goto cleanup;
+    }
+    if (!cudaOk(cudaMemcpy2D(a.data(), n * sizeof(double), deviceA, workN * sizeof(double),
+                             n * sizeof(double), n, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "factor download")) goto cleanup;
+    success = true;
+
+cleanup:
+    if (start) cudaEventDestroy(start);
+    if (stop) cudaEventDestroy(stop);
+    if (deviceFailed) cudaFree(deviceFailed);
+    if (deviceA) cudaFree(deviceA);
+    return success;
+}

+bool cudaOk(cudaError_t error, const char* operation) {

+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+__global__ void factorDiagonalBlock(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int width, int* failed) {

+    // this avoids global synchronization inside its dependency chain.

+__global__ void solvePanel(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    const size_t row = offset + diagonalWidth +
+                       static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (row >= n) return;
+    for (int j = 0; j < diagonalWidth; ++j) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (int p = 0; p < j; ++p)
+            sum += a[row * n + offset + p] * a[(offset + j) * n + offset + p];
+        a[row * n + offset + j] =
+            (a[row * n + offset + j] - sum) / a[(offset + j) * n + offset + j];
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void updateTrailing(double* a, size_t n, size_t offset, int diagonalWidth) {
+    // One 16x16 thread block updates a 32x32 output tile.  Each thread owns a
+    // 2x2 result, giving coalesced loads and enough arithmetic per thread.
+    __shared__ double left[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    __shared__ double right[kBlockSize][kBlockSize + 1];
+    const size_t base = offset + diagonalWidth;
+    const size_t tileRow = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * kBlockSize;
+    const size_t tileCol = base + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * kBlockSize;
+    if (tileRow < tileCol) return;
+
+    const int r0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y) * 2;
+    const int c0 = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) * 2;
+    const int rows[2] = {r0, r0 + 1};
+    const int cols[2] = {c0, c0 + 1};
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+            const size_t globalCol = offset + cc;
+            left[rr][cc] = (globalRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[globalRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+            const size_t rightRow = tileCol + rr;
+            right[rr][cc] = (rightRow < n && cc < diagonalWidth) ? a[rightRow * n + globalCol] : 0.0;
+        }
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (int rr : rows) {
+        const size_t globalRow = tileRow + rr;
+        if (globalRow >= n) continue;
+        for (int cc : cols) {
+            const size_t globalCol = tileCol + cc;
+            if (globalCol >= n || globalRow < globalCol) continue;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int p = 0; p < kBlockSize; ++p) sum += left[rr][p] * right[cc][p];
+            a[globalRow * n + globalCol] -= sum;
         }
     }
-    
-    return true;
